# Cleanup Inventory — 2026-07-13

Evidence + rollback record for repository de-tracking (`git rm --cached`).
Files are **only** removed from the git index; they remain on disk in the
working tree. No git history is rewritten.

## Method — how vendor vs authored was decided (no guessing)

The Iris pack de-tracking split was decided on three independent signals, not
on filename heuristics alone:

1. **Git history.** The entire `packs/overworld/` tree (4407 files) was added in
   a single vendor-import commit `f3a6856 chore: baseline server config-as-code`
   and **no project commit has ever modified a file inside it** since. The
   authored packs `packs/level_1/` and `packs/empty/` were touched by a
   *separate* project commit `17ec0de`.
2. **Upstream meta files.** `packs/overworld/` carries the upstream Iris pack
   repository's own metadata verbatim: `IrisDimensions-license.md`, `README.md`
   ("# Overworld V3000"), `Object-or-Jigsaw.md`, `Schema.json`,
   `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/`, and its own `.gitignore`. A project authoring its
   own pack would not recreate the vendor's issue templates and license.
3. **Authoring markers in the authored packs.** `packs/level_1/` and
   `packs/empty/` contain project artifacts absent from vendor packs:
   `AUTHORING.md`, `LOCH_LAYOUT_CONTRACT.md`, custom dungeon biomes
   (`dunstart/dunmid/dunend/void`), `build.gradle.kts`, and project map images.

Conclusion: `overworld` = 100% vendor default → **VENDOR_DEFAULT**.
`level_1` + `empty` = project content → **AUTHORED, stay tracked**.
`REVIEW_NEEDED` is **empty** (no file fell into the uncertain bucket).

## Reproducibility (how to restore the de-tracked vendor pack)

- Iris plugin JAR in use: **`Iris-3.9.1-1.20.1-1.21.11.jar`** (Iris 3.9.1),
  located at `MCMMORPG/plugins/` (not tracked; a plugin binary).
- Pack: **"Overworld V3000"** (`dimensions/*.json` → `"version": 31100`), the
  standard Iris overworld maintained by the IrisDimensions community.
- Restore via Iris in-game/console: `/iris download overworld` (Iris pulls the
  pack into `plugins/Iris/packs/overworld/`), or fetch the matching release from
  the IrisDimensions overworld pack repository. License terms: see the in-pack
  `IrisDimensions-license.md`.

## Set counts

| Set | Files | Action |
|-----|------:|--------|
| VENDOR_DEFAULT — `packs/overworld/` | 4407 | de-track (Task 1) |
| AUTHORED — `packs/level_1/` | 20 | keep tracked |
| AUTHORED — `packs/empty/` | 19 | keep tracked |
| KEEP — `Iris/settings.json`, `compat.json`, `compat.default.json`, `info/` | 6 | keep tracked |
| REVIEW_NEEDED | 0 | n/a |
| Tracked `.bak` (Task 2) | 21 | de-track |

Note: 16 of the 21 `.bak` files live inside `packs/overworld/` and are therefore
de-tracked with Task 1; Task 2 covers the remaining 5 under
`MythicDungeons/maps/level_1/` and adds a global `*.bak` ignore rule.

## Proposed `.gitignore` additions (Task 1)

    # ---- Iris vendor default pack (regenerable via `/iris download overworld`) ----
    MCMMORPG/plugins/Iris/packs/overworld/

Precise and safe: matches only `overworld/`. Authored `packs/level_1/`,
`packs/empty/`, `info/`, `settings.json`, `compat.json`, `compat.default.json`
are outside the pattern and stay tracked.
